================================================================================
Borkeo EMR Update Note: Version 42.3.0
Medicine names from the Cambodia Essential Medicines List in every prescription form
================================================================================

Date: 2026-10-22
Previous Version: 42.2.0
New Version: 42.3.0
Patient data: not touched. No database table change. Saved prescriptions are not
rewritten; a line changes only when a user saves the record again.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. WHAT WAS WRONG
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
a) The medicine name boxes offered only a short built-in list of about 60
   names. Doctors typed most medicines by hand, so names, strengths and forms
   were written differently each time, with no CMS code.
b) Every new row started with the number of medicine 1 and the route PO, so
   a medicine could be saved with a dose or route that nobody chose.
c) The units mg, mL, mcg, g and IU were shown as Khmer labels (ម.ក្រ, ម.ល,
   ម៉ៃក្រូក្រាម, ក្រាម, UI / IU) instead of the international units.
d) Each prescription table started with 5 empty rows.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2. WHAT CHANGED
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
a) Medicine search. Every medicine name box (OPD prescription, IPD admission
   prescription and protocol rows, daily note, discharge medicines) searches
   the Cambodia Essential Medicines List, 10th edition (2023-2024, 704
   medicines) after 2 letters. It matches the name, the CMS code, the form
   and the strength. Arrow up / down, Enter to choose, Esc to close. When
   nothing matches it shows: រកមិនឃើញឱសថ.
   A choice is written as: CMS code-Name-Form-Strength
     for example  AA0410, AA0411-Paracetamol (acetaminophen)-Tablet-500 mg
   (a medicine without a CMS code: Name-Form-Strength).
   Free text is still allowed. Rows added later with "+" work too.
b) After choosing: the number of medicine is cleared and gets the cursor; the
   unit is set from the medicine (tablets, capsules, suppositories: គ្រាប់;
   oral liquids, syrups and infusions: mL; injections: mg, mcg, IU, g or mmol
   from the strength, never ampoules; sachets and powders: កញ្ចប់; eye, ear
   and nose drops: ដំណក់; inhalers: បាញ់; creams, ointments, gels: g;
   patches, IUDs and implants: the new unit ដុំ). The route is never filled.
   The doctor can still change the unit.
c) Units: mg, mL, mcg, g and IU are written in the original units, not
   translated, in Khmer and in English. Old lines saved with the Khmer labels
   open as mg, mL, mcg, g, IU. Old and new forms count as the same unit in
   stock deduction, the ward medicine report, the maximum-dose warning, the
   nursing chart, the patient banner and printing.
d) New rows: the number of medicine and the route are blank (no default PO).
   A line with a medicine but no route cannot be saved; the message
   សូមជ្រើសរើសផ្លូវប្រើថ្នាំ appears next to the row. Empty rows are ignored.
   Old lines keep their saved route.
e) Each prescription table starts with 3 rows. Saved records still show every
   line (a record with 6 lines shows 6).
f) The CMS code of a chosen medicine is saved with the line (cmsCode). Old
   lines without it open and save exactly as before.
g) Discharge medicine names can be up to 600 characters (some list names,
   for example ORS, snake antivenoms and F-75 / F-100, are up to 515).

Not changed (by the owner's instruction): pharmacy, stock and drug-safety
logic. Stock is still matched by exact name and unit, so a medicine chosen
from the new list is deducted from stock only when a stock item has exactly
the same name. The OPD prescription sent to Pharmacy shows the full list name
(with the CMS code in it) but does not carry the separate cmsCode field.

Checked in a test copy with synthetic patients only, in Microsoft Edge
(tests/v423-medicine-list-browser.mjs): OPD prescription, IPD admission
prescription, daily note and discharge medicines - type "par", choose with
the keyboard, add a row with "+", type a dose, save, reopen, print; plus
Pharmacy dispensing and the nursing medication chart. The list of record
storage keys is the same as in v42.2.0 (checked with a saved record).
npm test: 915 of 915 pass. npm run check:khmer passes.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3. FILES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
New:
  server/catalogues/medicines-eml-2024.json  the 704 medicines (your file)
  server/medicine-catalogue.mjs     reads the list once
  server/medicine-units.mjs         old and new unit names are the same unit
  public/medicine-autocomplete.js   the medicine search (from your script)
  public/medicine-autocomplete.css  its look; the route warning
  public/medicine-units.js          the same unit table for the pages
  tests/v423-medicine-list.test.mjs          new tests
  tests/v423-medicine-list-browser.mjs       browser check (run separately)
Changed:
  protected/EMR.html                rows, units, blank route, 3 rows, CMS code,
                                    route check, old units shown as new
  public/emr-patient-record.js      route check when saving
  public/emr-inpatient-workflow.js  discharge medicines: route check, CMS
                                    code, units
  public/medication-safety.js       maximum dose: same unit
  public/patient-banner.js          medicines shown with the new units
  public/pmrs-import.js             unit list
  server/routes/clinical.mjs        GET /api/medicines/catalogue (login needed)
  server/server.mjs                 serves the new files; discharge cmsCode and
                                    600-character names; version comment v42.3
  server/stock.mjs                  unit alias only
  server/ward-medicine-matrix.mjs   unit alias only
  server/pmrs2-medicine-import.mjs  accepts the new units
  scripts/khmer-lib.mjs, docs/khmer-approved-changes.md
                                    the 5 international units may replace the
                                    old Khmer unit labels
  docs/translations-needed.csv, docs/approved-new-khmer.json,
  public/new-text-data.js, public/emr-language-data.js
                                    4 new Khmer texts you gave (រកមិនឃើញឱសថ,
                                    សូមជ្រើសរើសផ្លូវប្រើថ្នាំ, ដុំ, ដង)
  package.json                      version 42.3.0
  public/login.html                 shows v42.3
  CHANGELOG.md                      v42.3.0 entry
  tests/v1200-regression.test.mjs   checks the new list instead of the old one
  tests/accessibility-quality-smoke.test.mjs expects version 42.3.0

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
4. INSTALLATION (one step at a time)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The files are already updated in this folder. If you use this folder as is,
only steps 1 to 3 are needed.

Step 1: Close the EMR server window (or press Ctrl + C in it).
Step 2: Double-click "Start-Borkeo-WiFi.bat".
Step 3: In the browser press Ctrl + F5 once, open a patient's prescription and
        type "par" in a medicine name box. The list of medicines opens.

For Antigravity (only when applying to another copy of v42.2.0): apply the
code change in section 5 exactly (it keeps each file's line endings), then run
"npm test" and "npm run check:khmer".

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
5. CODE CHANGE (unified diff against v42.2.0)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 # Borkeo EMR Changelog
 
+## v42.3.0 (Medicine names from the Cambodia Essential Medicines List)
+- Every medicine name box in the prescription forms (OPD, IPD admission, IPD protocol rows, daily note, discharge medicines) searches the Cambodia Essential Medicines List, 10th edition (2023-2024, 704 medicines) after 2 letters. The search matches the name, the CMS code, the form and the strength; arrow keys, Enter and Esc work; the "no match" text is Khmer. A choice is filled as "CMS code-Name-Form-Strength" (without the code when the list has none). Free text is still allowed. The old short meds_list type-ahead was removed.
+- The list is served to logged-in staff (GET /api/medicines/catalogue, from server/catalogues/medicines-eml-2024.json) and searched in the browser (public/medicine-autocomplete.js and .css; no npm package, no server search while typing).
+- After choosing, the number of medicine is cleared and gets the focus, and the unit is set from the medicine (tablets, capsules, suppositories: គ្រាប់; oral liquids and infusions: mL; injections: mg, mcg, IU, g or mmol from the strength; sachets and powders: កញ្ចប់; drops: ដំណក់; inhalers: បាញ់; creams, ointments and gels: g; patches, IUDs and implants: the new unit ដុំ). The doctor can still change it.
+- Units: mg, mL, mcg, g and IU are written in the original international units, not translated (owner's order). Records saved with the old labels open as the new units; saved data is changed only when a user saves the record again. Old and new forms count as the same unit in stock deduction, the ward medicine report, the maximum-dose warning, the patient banner, the nursing chart and printing (public/medicine-units.js, server/medicine-units.mjs). The Khmer check allows exactly these five replacements.
+- The number of medicine and the route are blank in every new row (no default PO). A line with a medicine but no route cannot be saved ("សូមជ្រើសរើសផ្លូវប្រើថ្នាំ" next to the row); empty rows are ignored. Old lines keep their saved route.
+- Each prescription table starts with 3 rows (5 before). Saved records still show every line. Two hidden placeholder rows keep the record storage positions of the old daily-note rows 4 and 5, so no saved field key changes.
+- The CMS code of a chosen medicine is saved with the line (cmsCode, additive; old lines are unchanged). Discharge medicine names can be up to 600 characters (some list names are up to 515).
+- Tests: tests/v423-medicine-list.test.mjs (new); tests/v423-medicine-list-browser.mjs (new, explicit browser check); tests/v1200-regression.test.mjs checks the new list instead of the removed datalist; tests/accessibility-quality-smoke.test.mjs expects 42.3.0. npm run check:khmer passes.
+
 ## v42.2.0 (Start-Borkeo-WiFi.bat no longer closes at once)
 - Start-Borkeo-WiFi.bat called npm without "call", so when the server could not start, the window closed before the error could be read. It now starts Node directly (Node is also found in C:\Program Files\nodejs when it is not on the PATH), and first checks the usual problems with a clear message: the EMR files are not next to the file (ZIP not extracted), Node.js is not installed, or port 3001 is already in use (the EMR is already running). On any error the window stays open.
 - Tests: 903/903 (tests/accessibility-quality-smoke.test.mjs expects 42.2.0). npm run check:khmer passes.

+## v42.3: medication units written as international units (owner's order)
+
+The owner ordered that mg, mL, mcg, g and IU are written in the original
+international units, not translated, in the medication unit lists
+(protected/EMR.html unit selects and their EN/KH label table, public/pmrs-import.js).
+A Khmer unit label listed here may disappear only when it is replaced by one of
+these five units (scripts/khmer-lib.mjs INTERNATIONAL_UNITS). Records saved with
+the old labels still open as the new units (public/medicine-units.js).
+
+| file | line | before | after |
+|------|------|--------|-------|
+| protected/EMR.html, public/pmrs-import.js | unit lists | ម.ក្រ (mg) | mg |
+| protected/EMR.html, public/pmrs-import.js | unit lists | ម.ក្រ | mg |
+| protected/EMR.html, public/pmrs-import.js | unit lists | ម.ល (ml) | mL |
+| protected/EMR.html, public/pmrs-import.js | unit lists | ម.ល | mL |
+| protected/EMR.html, public/pmrs-import.js | unit lists | ម៉ៃក្រូក្រាម (mcg/µg) | mcg |
+| protected/EMR.html, public/pmrs-import.js | unit lists | ម៉ៃក្រូក្រាម | mcg |
+| protected/EMR.html, public/pmrs-import.js | unit lists | ក្រាម (g) | g |

+    const message = window.getBorkeoLanguage?.() === 'en' ? 'Choose the route' : 'សូមជ្រើសរើសផ្លូវប្រើថ្នាំ';

diff --git a/public/medicine-autocomplete.js b/public/medicine-autocomplete.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..f09e53e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/public/medicine-autocomplete.js
@@ -0,0 +1,200 @@
+/*! Borkeo medicine search (v42.3) - dependency-free, in-memory search of the
+ *  Cambodia Essential Medicines List (704 items, under 1 ms per keystroke).
+ *  Based on the owner's medicine-autocomplete.js; adapted to the EMR:
+ *  - the list is loaded once from GET /api/medicines/catalogue (login needed);
+ *  - styles live in /public/medicine-autocomplete.css (no inline style element);
+ *  - choosing a medicine fills "CMS code-Name-Form-Strength", clears the
+ *    number of medicine, sets the unit and fires the existing row handlers;
+ *  - the route is never filled; free text stays allowed.
+ *  Works for rows added later (event delegation). */
+(function (root) {
+  'use strict';
+  var DATA = [], READY = false, LOADING = false, queue = [], active = null;
+  var DATA_URL = '/api/medicines/catalogue';
+
+  // Medicine name boxes of every prescription form (EMR.html rows and the
+  // discharge medicines in emr-inpatient-workflow.js).
+  var INPUT_SELECTOR = '.rx-name, .mrx-name, .orx-name, .prx-name, .mprx-name, #emr-discharge-rx-body [data-med="name"]';
+
+  // Catalogue unit -> unit option value (owner's rules, v42.3).
+  var UNIT_OPTION = { 'mg': 'mg', 'mL': 'mL', 'mcg': 'mcg', 'g': 'g', 'IU': 'IU', 'mmol': 'mmol',
+    'គ្រាប់': 'គ្រាប់', 'កញ្ចប់': 'កញ្ចប់', 'ដំណក់': 'ដំណក់', 'ដង': 'បាញ់', 'ក្រាម': 'g', 'ដុំ': 'ដុំ' };
+
+  function optionUnit(item) {
+    var unit = String(item && item.unit || '');
+    return UNIT_OPTION[unit] || unit;
+  }
+
+  function english() {
+    try { return typeof root.getBorkeoLanguage === 'function' && root.getBorkeoLanguage() === 'en'; } catch (e) { return false; }
+  }
+
+  function norm(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); }
+  function esc(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]; }); }
+
+  function load(items) {
+    DATA = items.map(function (m) {
+      m._code = norm(m.code); m._name = norm(m.name);
+      m._words = norm(m.name + ' ' + m.form + ' ' + m.strength).split(/[^a-z0-9µ.%+]+/).filter(Boolean);
+      m._key = norm(m.code + ' ' + m.name + ' ' + m.form + ' ' + m.strength);
+      return m;
+    });
+    READY = true; queue.splice(0).forEach(function (f) { f(); });
+  }
+
+  function ensureData() {
+    if (READY || LOADING) return;
+    LOADING = true;
+    fetch(DATA_URL, { credentials: 'same-origin', cache: 'force-cache' })
+      .then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('catalogue ' + r.status); return r.json(); })
+      .then(function (body) { load(body.items || []); })
+      .catch(function () { LOADING = false; queue.length = 0; });
+  }
+
+  // Score: lower = better. Every typed token must match; null = no match.
+  function search(query, max) {
+    var q = norm(query); if (q.length < 2) return [];
+    var toks = q.split(' '), out = [];
+    for (var i = 0; i < DATA.length; i++) {
+      var m = DATA[i], score = 0, ok = true;
+      for (var t = 0; t < toks.length; t++) {
+        var tk = toks[t], s;
+        if (m._code && m._code.indexOf(tk) === 0) s = 0;
+        else if (m._name.indexOf(tk) === 0) s = 1;
+        else if (m._words.some(function (w) { return w.indexOf(tk) === 0; })) s = t === 0 ? 2 : 1;
+        else if (tk.length >= 3 && m._key.indexOf(tk) > -1) s = 4;
+        else { ok = false; break; }
+        score += s;
+      }
+      if (ok) out.push([score, m]);
+    }
+    out.sort(function (a, b) { return a[0] - b[0] || (a[1]._name < b[1]._name ? -1 : a[1]._name > b[1]._name ? 1 : 0) || a[1].id - b[1].id; });
+    return out.slice(0, max || 12).map(function (x) { return x[1]; });
+  }
+
+  function highlight(text, query) {
+    var toks = norm(query).split(' ').filter(Boolean), out = esc(text);
+    toks.forEach(function (tk) {
+      var re = new RegExp('(' + tk.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') + ')', 'ig');
+      out = out.replace(/(<[^>]+>)|([^<]+)/g, function (_, tag, txt) { return tag || txt.replace(re, '<mark>$1</mark>'); });
+    });
+    return out;
+  }
+
+  function closeList() { if (active && active.el.parentNode) active.el.parentNode.removeChild(active.el); active = null; }
+
+  // The list sits under its name box; it follows the box when the page or a
+  // table scrolls and closes only when the box leaves the screen.
+  function place(el, input) {
+    var r = input.getBoundingClientRect();
+    el.style.left = Math.max(4, Math.min(r.left, window.innerWidth - 444)) + 'px';
+    el.style.top = (r.bottom + 4) + 'px';
+    el.style.width = Math.max(r.width, 420) + 'px';
+    return r.bottom > 0 && r.top < window.innerHeight;
+  }
+
+  function openList(input) {
+    var res = search(input.value, 12);
+    closeList();
+    if (input.value.trim().length < 2) return;
+    var el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'bk-med-list no-print'; el.setAttribute('role', 'listbox');
+    el.setAttribute('data-language-exclude', '');
+    place(el, input);
+    if (!res.length) el.innerHTML = '<div class="bk-med-empty">' + (english() ? 'No matching medicine' : 'រកមិនឃើញឱសថ') + '</div>';
+    else el.innerHTML = res.map(function (m, i) {
+      return '<div class="bk-med-item' + (i === 0 ? ' on' : '') + '" role="option" data-i="' + i + '">' +
+        '<span class="bk-med-label">' + highlight(m.label, input.value) + '</span>' +
+        '<span class="bk-med-unit">' + esc(optionUnit(m)) + '</span></div>';
+    }).join('');
+    document.body.appendChild(el);
+    active = { el: el, input: input, res: res, idx: res.length ? 0 : -1 };
+    el.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) {
+      var it = e.target.closest('.bk-med-item'); if (!it) return;
+      e.preventDefault(); choose(res[+it.dataset.i], input);
+    });
+  }
+
+  function move(d) {
+    if (!active || !active.res.length) return;
+    var items = active.el.querySelectorAll('.bk-med-item');
+    items[active.idx].classList.remove('on');
+    active.idx = (active.idx + d + items.length) % items.length;
+    items[active.idx].classList.add('on'); items[active.idx].scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
+  }
+
+  function fire(el, type) { if (el) el.dispatchEvent(new Event(type, { bubbles: true })); }
+
+  function setUnit(control, unit) {
+    if (!control || !unit) return;
+    if (control.tagName === 'SELECT') {
+      var found = Array.prototype.some.call(control.options, function (o) { return o.value === unit; });
+      if (!found) control.add(new Option(unit, unit));
+      control.value = unit;
+    } else {
+      control.value = unit;
+    }
+    fire(control, 'input');
+    fire(control, 'change');
+  }
+
+  function rowParts(input) {
+    var row = input.closest('tr') || input.parentElement;
+    if (input.matches('[data-med="name"]')) {
+      return { row: row, qty: row.querySelector('[data-med="doseAmount"]'), unit: row.querySelector('[data-med="unit"]') };
+    }
+    var prefix = (/\b(rx|mrx|orx|prx|mprx)-name\b/.exec(input.className) || [])[1] || 'rx';
+    return { row: row, qty: row.querySelector('.' + prefix + '-dose'), unit: row.querySelector('.' + prefix + '-unit') };
+  }
+
+  function choose(m, input) {
+    var parts = rowParts(input);
+    input.value = m.label;
+    input.dataset.cmsCode = m.code || '';
+    input.dataset.medicineId = String(m.id);
+    if (parts.qty) parts.qty.value = '';            // number of medicine: blank by default
+    setUnit(parts.unit, optionUnit(m));
+    fire(input, 'change');
+    if (parts.qty) fire(parts.qty, 'input');         // totals, legacy fields, nursing orders
+    input.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('medicine:selected', { bubbles: true, detail: m }));
+    closeList();
+    if (parts.qty) parts.qty.focus();
+  }
+
+  function init() {
+    document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
+      var target = e.target;
+      if (!target || !target.matches || !target.matches(INPUT_SELECTOR)) return;
+      // Typed by hand: no longer the chosen list medicine (free text is allowed).
+      delete target.dataset.cmsCode;
+      delete target.dataset.medicineId;
+      ensureData();
+      var go = function () { if (document.activeElement === target) openList(target); };
+      READY ? go() : queue.push(go);
+    });
+    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
+      if (!active || !e.target.matches || !e.target.matches(INPUT_SELECTOR)) return;
+      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') { e.preventDefault(); move(1); }
+      else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') { e.preventDefault(); move(-1); }
+      else if (e.key === 'Enter' && active.idx > -1) { e.preventDefault(); choose(active.res[active.idx], active.input); }
+      else if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); closeList(); }
+    });
+    // Close only the list of the box that lost focus (moving to another name box
+    // must not close the list just opened there).
+    document.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) {
+      var target = e.target;
+      if (!target.matches || !target.matches(INPUT_SELECTOR)) return;
+      setTimeout(function () { if (active && active.input === target && document.activeElement !== target) closeList(); }, 120);
+    });
+    window.addEventListener('scroll', function (e) {
+      if (!active || active.el.contains(e.target)) return;
+      if (!place(active.el, active.input)) closeList();
+    }, true);
+    window.addEventListener('resize', function () { if (active && !place(active.el, active.input)) closeList(); });
+  }
+
+  root.BorkeoMedicine = { search: search, load: load, optionUnit: optionUnit, choose: choose, INPUT_SELECTOR: INPUT_SELECTOR };
+  if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
+    if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init, { once: true });
+    else init();
+  }
+})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);
diff --git a/public/medicine-units.js b/public/medicine-units.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..0e72838
--- /dev/null
+++ b/public/medicine-units.js
@@ -0,0 +1,28 @@
+/* v42.3: medication units (same table as server/medicine-units.mjs).
+ * Prescription forms write mg, mL, mcg, g and IU (international units, never
+ * translated). Records saved before v42.3 hold the old Khmer labels; they are
+ * shown and compared as the new units. Saved data is never rewritten here. */
+(function (root) {
+  'use strict';
+  var ALIASES = {
+    'ម.ក្រ': 'mg',
+    'ម.ល': 'mL',
+    'ml': 'mL',
+    'ម៉ៃក្រូក្រាម': 'mcg',
+    'µg': 'mcg',
+    'ក្រាម': 'g',
+    'ui': 'IU',
+    'iu': 'IU'
+  };
+
+  function canonical(value) {
+    var unit = String(value == null ? '' : value).normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
+    return ALIASES[unit] || ALIASES[unit.toLowerCase()] || unit;
+  }
+
+  function same(left, right) {
+    return canonical(left).toLowerCase() === canonical(right).toLowerCase();
+  }
+
+  root.BorkeoMedicineUnits = Object.freeze({ canonical: canonical, same: same });
+})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

   "form.newEntry": "ទម្រង់ថ្មី",
   "form.print": "បោះពុម្ព",
   "shell.switchUserType": "ប្តូរប្រភេទអ្នកប្រើ",
+  "medicine.noMatch": "រកមិនឃើញឱសថ",
+  "medicine.routeRequired": "សូមជ្រើសរើសផ្លូវប្រើថ្នាំ",
+  "medicine.unitPiece": "ដុំ",
+  "medicine.cataloguePuff": "ដង",
 };

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..415fc4f

+++ b/server/medicine-catalogue.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,13 @@
+import fs from 'node:fs';
+
+// v42.3: Cambodia Essential Medicines List, 10th edition (2023-2024), 704 items.
+// Read once and served to logged-in staff; the browser searches it locally.
+let cached = null;
+
+export function medicineCatalogue() {
+  if (!cached) {
+    const items = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL('./catalogues/medicines-eml-2024.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'));
+    cached = Object.freeze({ source: 'Cambodia Essential Medicines List, 10th edition (2023-2024)', count: items.length, items });
+  }
+  return cached;
+}
diff --git a/server/medicine-units.mjs b/server/medicine-units.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b6d8e11
--- /dev/null
+++ b/server/medicine-units.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,23 @@
+// v42.3: medication units. The prescription forms now write mg, mL, mcg, g and
+// IU (international units, never translated). Records saved before v42.3 hold
+// the old Khmer labels. Both forms count as the same unit wherever a unit is
+// compared (stock, ward medicine report columns); saved data is never rewritten.
+const UNIT_ALIASES = Object.freeze({
+  'ម.ក្រ': 'mg',
+  'ម.ល': 'mL',
+  'ml': 'mL',
+  'ម៉ៃក្រូក្រាម': 'mcg',
+  'µg': 'mcg',
+  'ក្រាម': 'g',
+  'ui': 'IU',
+  'iu': 'IU',
+});
+
+export function canonicalMedicineUnit(value) {
+  const unit = String(value ?? '').normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
+  return UNIT_ALIASES[unit] || UNIT_ALIASES[unit.toLowerCase()] || unit;
+}
+
+export function sameMedicineUnit(left, right) {
+  return canonicalMedicineUnit(left).toLowerCase() === canonicalMedicineUnit(right).toLowerCase();
+}

+// v42.3 - Medicine names from the Cambodia Essential Medicines List (10th edition,
+// 2023-2024) in every prescription form; units written as international units;
+// blank number of medicine and route; 3 starting rows; CMS code saved.
+// The browser behaviour is also checked in tests/v423-medicine-list-browser.mjs.
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import crypto from 'node:crypto';
+import fs from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import test from 'node:test';
+import vm from 'node:vm';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { createEmrServer } from '../server/server.mjs';
+import { openDatabase } from '../server/database.mjs';
+import { ensureStockSchema, addStockItem, listStock, syncMedicineStock } from '../server/stock.mjs';
+import { canonicalMedicineUnit, sameMedicineUnit } from '../server/medicine-units.mjs';
+import { buildMedicineMatrix } from '../server/ward-medicine-matrix.mjs';
+import { checkAgainstBaseline } from '../scripts/khmer-lib.mjs';
+
+const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+// Line endings differ between checkouts (CRLF on Windows); compare with LF.
+const read = file => fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, file), 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
+const catalogue = JSON.parse(read('server/catalogues/medicines-eml-2024.json'));
+const html = read('protected/EMR.html');
+// Unit labels written before v42.3 (now mg, mL, mcg, g, IU).
+const OLD_UNIT_LABELS = ['ម.ក្រ', 'ម.ល', 'ម៉ៃក្រូក្រាម', 'ក្រាម', 'UI'];
+
+function browserScripts() {
+  const context = vm.createContext({ console });
+  vm.runInContext(read('public/medicine-units.js'), context);
+  vm.runInContext(read('public/medicine-autocomplete.js'), context);
+  context.BorkeoMedicine.load(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(catalogue)));
+  return context;
+}
+
+test('the catalogue has 704 medicines, each with a label and a unit', () => {
+  assert.equal(catalogue.length, 704);
+  for (const item of catalogue) {
+    assert.ok(item.label && item.unit && item.name, `item ${item.id}`);
+    const expected = [item.code, item.name, item.form, item.strength].filter(Boolean).join('-');
+    assert.equal(item.label, expected, `label of item ${item.id} is CMS code-Name-Form-Strength`);
+  }
+  assert.equal(catalogue.find(item => item.label.startsWith('AA0410')).label, 'AA0410, AA0411-Paracetamol (acetaminophen)-Tablet-500 mg');
+  assert.ok(catalogue.some(item => !item.code && item.label === 'Isoflurane-Inhalation'), 'a medicine without a CMS code is shown without it');
+});
+
+test('search: "par" finds Paracetamol, "AA04" finds the AA04 codes, "amox" finds Amoxicillin', () => {
+  const { BorkeoMedicine } = browserScripts();
+  assert.deepEqual(Array.from(BorkeoMedicine.search('p')), [], 'search starts after 2 letters');
+  assert.ok(BorkeoMedicine.search('par').some(item => item.name.startsWith('Paracetamol')));
+  const codes = BorkeoMedicine.search('AA04', 50);
+  assert.ok(codes.length >= 10);
+  assert.ok(codes.every(item => item.code.startsWith('AA04')));
+  assert.ok(BorkeoMedicine.search('amox').length > 0);
+  assert.ok(BorkeoMedicine.search('amox').every(item => item.name.startsWith('Amoxicillin')));
+  assert.ok(BorkeoMedicine.search('paracetamol tablet').every(item => /tablet/i.test(item.form)), 'name and form both match');
+  assert.ok(BorkeoMedicine.search('fentanyl 50').some(item => /50 micrograms/.test(item.strength)), 'strength matches');
+});
+
+test('choosing a medicine sets the unit by the owner rules (international units untranslated)', () => {
+  const { BorkeoMedicine } = browserScripts();
+  const unitOf = id => BorkeoMedicine.optionUnit(catalogue.find(item => item.id === id));
+  assert.equal(unitOf(22), 'គ្រាប់', 'Midazolam tablet');
+  assert.equal(unitOf(21), 'mL', 'Midazolam oral liquid');
+  assert.equal(unitOf(5), 'mg', 'Ketamine injection 50 mg');
+  assert.equal(unitOf(44), 'mcg', 'Fentanyl injection 50 micrograms');
+  assert.equal(unitOf(172), 'IU', 'Benzathine benzylpenicillin 1.2 million IU');
+  assert.equal(unitOf(292), 'g', 'Clotrimazole cream');
+  assert.equal(unitOf(643), 'បាញ់', 'inhaler: puff');
+  assert.equal(unitOf(41), 'ដុំ', 'transdermal patch: piece');
+  const allowed = new Set(['mg', 'mL', 'mcg', 'g', 'IU', 'mmol', 'គ្រាប់', 'កញ្ចប់', 'ដំណក់', 'បាញ់', 'ដុំ']);
+  for (const item of catalogue) assert.ok(allowed.has(BorkeoMedicine.optionUnit(item)), `${item.label}: ${item.unit}`);
+  for (const item of catalogue.filter(entry => entry.kind === 'inj')) {
+    assert.ok(!['អំពូល', 'ផ្លាកុង'].includes(BorkeoMedicine.optionUnit(item)), 'injections are never in ampoules');
+  }
+});
+
+test('unit selects show mg, mL, mcg, g and IU, never the old Khmer labels; one new piece unit', () => {
+  const selects = [...html.matchAll(/<select class="rx-select [^"]*-unit"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/select>/g)].map(match => match[1]);
+  assert.equal(selects.length, 3, 'two row templates and the daily-note starter row');
+  for (const options of selects) {
+    for (const unit of ['mg', 'mL', 'mcg', 'g', 'IU', 'ដុំ']) assert.match(options, new RegExp(`<option value="${unit}">${unit}</option>`));
+    for (const old of OLD_UNIT_LABELS) assert.equal(options.includes(old), false, old);
+    const values = [...options.matchAll(/value="([^"]*)"/g)].map(match => match[1]);
+    assert.equal(new Set(values).size, values.length, 'no duplicate units');
+  }
+  assert.match(html, /"mg": \{ khmer: "mg", english: "mg" \}/, 'the unit is not translated in either language');
+  assert.match(html, /"IU": \{ khmer: "IU", english: "IU" \}/);
+  assert.match(read('public/pmrs-import.js'), /'ដុំ','mL','mg','mcg','g','លីត្រ','IU'/);
+});
+
+test('an old line saved with the Khmer mg label opens as mg, and stock still matches it', () => {
+  const { BorkeoMedicineUnits } = browserScripts();
+  for (const [old, unit] of [['ម.ក្រ', 'mg'], ['ម.ល', 'mL'], ['ម៉ៃក្រូក្រាម', 'mcg'], ['ក្រាម', 'g'], ['UI', 'IU'], ['គ្រាប់', 'គ្រាប់']]) {
+    assert.equal(BorkeoMedicineUnits.canonical(old), unit);
+    assert.equal(canonicalMedicineUnit(old), unit);
+  }
+  assert.ok(sameMedicineUnit('ម.ក្រ', 'mg') && !sameMedicineUnit('mg', 'mL'));
+  assert.match(html, /put\('\.' \+ prefix \+ '-' \+ part, part === 'dose' \? med\.doseAmount : \(part === 'unit' && med\.unit \? medicationUnitValue\(med\.unit\)/);
+
+  const db = openDatabase(':memory:');
+  try {
+    ensureStockSchema(db);
+    addStockItem(db, 'medicine', { name: 'Diazepam injection', unit: 'ម.ក្រ', quantity: 100 }, 'pharmacist');
+    addStockItem(db, 'medicine', { name: 'Morphine injection', unit: 'mg', quantity: 100 }, 'pharmacist');
+    syncMedicineStock(db, 'IPD:new-unit', [{ name: 'Diazepam injection', unit: 'mg', total: 10 }], 'doctor');
+    syncMedicineStock(db, 'IPD:old-unit', [{ name: 'Morphine injection', unit: 'ម.ក្រ', total: 5 }], 'doctor');
+    const balance = name => listStock(db, 'medicine').find(item => item.name === name).balance;
+    assert.equal(balance('Diazepam injection'), 90);
+    assert.equal(balance('Morphine injection'), 95);
+    assert.throws(() => syncMedicineStock(db, 'IPD:wrong', [{ name: 'Morphine injection', unit: 'mL', total: 1 }], 'doctor'), /Stock unit does not match/);
+  } finally { db.sqlite.close(); }
+
+  const matrix = buildMedicineMatrix([{ patientKey: 'a', medications: [{ name: 'Diazepam 10 mg', unit: 'ម.ក្រ', total: '2' }, { name: 'Diazepam 10 mg', unit: 'mg', total: '3' }] }]);
+  assert.equal(matrix.columns.length, 1, 'ward medicine report: old and new unit forms are one column');
+  const safety = read('public/medication-safety.js');
+  assert.match(safety, /normalize\(canonicalUnit\(rule\.unit\)\) !== doses\.unit/, 'maximum dose compares the same unit');
+});

+test('catalogue, script and styles are served; the catalogue needs a login; CMS code is saved; free text and old lines stay', async () => {
+  const app = createEmrServer({ demoMode: true, databasePath: ':memory:', host: '127.0.0.1', port: 0, appOrigin: 'http://127.0.0.1:0', nodeEnv: 'test' });

+    const response = await fetch(origin + '/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { Origin: app.config.appOrigin, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ username }) });
+    assert.equal(response.status, 200);
+    const Cookie = response.headers.get('set-cookie').split(';')[0];
+    const me = await (await fetch(origin + '/api/auth/me', { headers: { Cookie } })).json();
+    return { Cookie, Origin: app.config.appOrigin, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': me.csrfToken };
+  };
+  try {
+    assert.equal((await fetch(origin + '/api/medicines/catalogue')).status, 401, 'login needed');
+    const doctor = await login('doctor_medicine');
+    const served = await fetch(origin + '/api/medicines/catalogue', { headers: doctor });
+    assert.equal(served.status, 200);
+    const body = await served.json();

+    assert.equal(body.items.length, 704);
+    for (const [file, type] of [['medicine-autocomplete.js', 'javascript'], ['medicine-autocomplete.css', 'css'], ['medicine-units.js', 'javascript']]) {
+      const response = await fetch(`${origin}/public/${file}`);
+      assert.equal(response.status, 200, file);
+      assert.match(response.headers.get('content-type'), new RegExp(type));
+    }
+    const page = await (await fetch(origin + '/emr?view=medical', { headers: doctor })).text();
+    assert.match(page, /\/public\/medicine-autocomplete\.js/);
+    assert.match(page, /\/public\/medicine-autocomplete\.css/);
+    assert.match(page, /\/public\/medicine-units\.js/);
+
+    // Saved medication lines: cmsCode is additive; old lines and free text unchanged.
+    const today = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'Asia/Phnom_Penh' }).format(new Date());
+    const oldLine = { rowType: 'regularRow', name: 'Paracetamol 500 mg', route: 'po', unit: 'ម.ក្រ', m: '1', a: '0', e: '1', n: '0', d: '5', total: '10', note: '', doseAmount: '1', scheduleType: 'bid' };
+    const chosen = { ...oldLine, name: 'AA0040-Amoxicillin-powder for suspension (oral liquid)-125 mg (as trihydrate)/5 mL', unit: 'mL', doseAmount: '', cmsCode: 'AA0040' };
+    const freeText = { ...oldLine, name: 'Synthetic medicine not in the list', unit: 'គ្រាប់' };
+    const medications = [oldLine, chosen, freeText];
+    const saved = await fetch(origin + '/api/patient-record', { method: 'PUT', headers: doctor, body: JSON.stringify({
+      patientKey: 'case:V423-1', editorScope: 'medical',
+      summary: { caseNumber: 'V423-1', healthId: '', firstName: 'Synthetic', lastName: 'Medicine', fullName: 'Synthetic Medicine', sex: 'ប្រុស', age: '30', departmentCode: 'medicine', departmentNameKh: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺទូទៅ', departmentNameEn: 'Medicine', serviceMode: 'ipd', entryDate: today },
+      data: { 'scope:medical:id:ipd_first_exam_date': today, 'system:initialIpdMedications': JSON.stringify(medications) },
+    }) });
+    assert.equal(saved.status, 200, await saved.clone().text());
+    const stored = JSON.parse(app.database.findPatient('case:V423-1').record_json)['system:initialIpdMedications'];
+    assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(stored), medications, 'saved exactly: old line unchanged, cmsCode kept, free text kept');
+    assert.equal('cmsCode' in JSON.parse(stored)[0], false, 'an old line gets no cmsCode');
+
+    // The ward medicine report reads the long names and both unit forms.
+    const director = await login('director');
+    const report = await (await fetch(`${origin}/api/reports/ward-medicines?ward=medicine&date=${today}`, { headers: director })).json();
+    assert.equal(report.matrix.patientCount, 1);
+    assert.ok(report.matrix.columns.some(column => column.name.startsWith('AA0040-Amoxicillin')));

+    await new Promise(resolve => app.server.close(resolve));
+    app.database.close();
+  }

+// v42.3 browser check (explicit, like tests/v378-encounter-browser.mjs; not part of npm test):
+//   node --test tests/v423-medicine-list-browser.mjs
+// Synthetic patients only, in-memory database. For the OPD prescription, the IPD
+// admission prescription, the daily note and the discharge medicines: type "par",
+// choose with the keyboard, add a row with "+", type a dose, save, reopen, print.
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import crypto from 'node:crypto';
+import fs from 'node:fs';
+import os from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { createEmrServer } from '../server/server.mjs';
+
+const sleep = ms => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
+
+async function openBrowser() {
+  const executable = [process.env.EMR_TEST_BROWSER, 'C:/Program Files (x86)/Microsoft/Edge/Application/msedge.exe', 'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe']
+    .find(file => file && fs.existsSync(file));
+  assert.ok(executable, 'Set EMR_TEST_BROWSER to run this explicit Chromium test.');
+  const debugPort = 9400 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 400);
+  const profile = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'borkeo-v423-'));
+  const child = spawn(executable, ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--no-first-run', `--remote-debugging-port=${debugPort}`, `--user-data-dir=${profile}`, 'about:blank'], { stdio: 'ignore' });
+  let url;
+  for (let i = 0; i < 80 && !url; i++) {
+    try { url = (await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${debugPort}/json/list`)).json()).find(item => item.type === 'page')?.webSocketDebuggerUrl; } catch {}
+    if (!url) await sleep(250);
+  }
+  const ws = new WebSocket(url);
+  await new Promise(resolve => ws.addEventListener('open', resolve, { once: true }));
+  let seq = 0;
+  const pending = new Map();
+  const state = { loads: 0, errors: [] };
+  ws.addEventListener('message', event => {
+    const message = JSON.parse(event.data);
+    if (message.id && pending.has(message.id)) { pending.get(message.id)(message); pending.delete(message.id); return; }
+    if (message.method === 'Page.loadEventFired') state.loads++;
+    if (message.method === 'Runtime.exceptionThrown') state.errors.push(message.params.exceptionDetails?.exception?.description || message.params.exceptionDetails?.text);
+    if (message.method === 'Page.javascriptDialogOpening') send('Page.handleJavaScriptDialog', { accept: true }).catch(() => {});
+  });
+  const send = (method, params = {}) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const id = ++seq;
+    pending.set(id, message => message.error ? reject(new Error(`${method}: ${message.error.message}`)) : resolve(message.result));
+    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params }));
+  });
+  await send('Page.enable'); await send('Runtime.enable'); await send('Network.enable');
+  await send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: 1440, height: 900, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: false });
+  const evaluate = async expression => {
+    const result = await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression, awaitPromise: true, returnByValue: true });
+    if (result.exceptionDetails) throw new Error(result.exceptionDetails.exception?.description || result.exceptionDetails.text);
+    return result.result.value;
+  };
+  const waitFor = async (expression, label, timeout = 20000) => {
+    const until = Date.now() + timeout;
+    while (Date.now() < until) { try { if (await evaluate(expression)) return; } catch {} await sleep(150); }
+    throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${label}`);
+  };
+  const key = async (keyName, code) => {
+    const keyCode = { ArrowDown: 40, ArrowUp: 38, Enter: 13, Escape: 27 }[keyName];
+    await send('Input.dispatchKeyEvent', { type: 'rawKeyDown', key: keyName, code: code || keyName, windowsVirtualKeyCode: keyCode });
+    await send('Input.dispatchKeyEvent', { type: 'keyUp', key: keyName, code: code || keyName, windowsVirtualKeyCode: keyCode });
+  };
+  const type = text => send('Input.insertText', { text });
+  const close = () => { ws.close(); child.kill(); };
+  return { send, evaluate, waitFor, key, type, close, state };
+}
+
+test('v42.3 medicine list in the OPD, IPD admission, daily note and discharge prescriptions', { timeout: 300000 }, async () => {
+  const app = createEmrServer({ demoMode: true, databasePath: ':memory:', host: '127.0.0.1', port: 0, appOrigin: 'http://127.0.0.1:0', nodeEnv: 'test' });
+  await new Promise(resolve => app.server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve));
+  const origin = `http://127.0.0.1:${app.server.address().port}`;
+  app.config.appOrigin = origin;
+  const db = app.database;
+  const browser = await openBrowser();
+  const today = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'Asia/Phnom_Penh' }).format(new Date());
+  const api = async (username) => {
+    const response = await fetch(origin + '/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { Origin: origin, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ username }) });
+    const Cookie = response.headers.get('set-cookie').split(';')[0];
+    const me = await (await fetch(origin + '/api/auth/me', { headers: { Cookie } })).json();
+    return { Cookie, Origin: origin, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': me.csrfToken };
+  };
+  const useAccount = async username => {
+    const headers = await api(username);
+    const [name, value] = headers.Cookie.split('=');
+    await browser.send('Network.clearBrowserCookies');
+    await browser.send('Network.setCookie', { name, value, url: origin });
+  };
+  const open = async (pathname, ready) => {
+    const before = browser.state.loads;
+    await browser.send('Page.navigate', { url: origin + pathname });
+    const until = Date.now() + 30000;
+    while (browser.state.loads === before && Date.now() < until) await sleep(100);
+    await browser.waitFor(ready, pathname);
+    await sleep(1500);

+    await browser.evaluate(`window.__printed=[];window.printEmrReport=async(o)=>{window.__printed.push((o.selectors||[]).map(s=>[...document.querySelectorAll(s)].map(e=>e.innerText).join('\\n')).join('\\n'));};true`);

+  // 2nd list entry with the keyboard.

+    } catch (error) {

+      throw new Error(`${error.message}: ${why}`);
+    }
+    const second = await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelectorAll('.bk-med-list .bk-med-item')[1].querySelector('.bk-med-label').innerText`);
+    await browser.key('ArrowDown');
+    await browser.key('Enter');
+    await browser.waitFor(`!document.querySelector('.bk-med-list')`, 'list closed');
+    return second;
+  };
+  const rowState = (rowSelector, prefix) => browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const rows=[...document.querySelectorAll(${JSON.stringify(rowSelector)})];return rows.map(r=>{
+      const q=s=>r.querySelector(s);const name=q(${JSON.stringify(prefix ? `.${prefix}-name` : '[data-med="name"]')}),dose=q(${JSON.stringify(prefix ? `.${prefix}-dose` : '[data-med="doseAmount"]')}),
+      unit=q(${JSON.stringify(prefix ? `.${prefix}-unit` : '[data-med="unit"]')}),route=q(${JSON.stringify(prefix ? `.${prefix}-route` : '[data-med="route"]')}),total=q(${JSON.stringify(prefix ? `.${prefix}-total` : '[data-med="total"]')});
+      return {name:name?.value||'',cms:name?.dataset.cmsCode||'',dose:dose?.value??'',unit:unit?.value||'',route:route?.value??'',total:total?.value??'',focused:document.activeElement===dose,
+        routeMessage:r.querySelector('.medication-route-message')?.textContent||''}})})()`);
+  const labelOf = text => text.trim();
+  // A record may open in view mode; the doctor presses Edit (v37.8).
+  const ensureEditMode = async (controlSelector) => {
+    const editable = `(()=>{const c=document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(controlSelector)});return Boolean(c&&!c.disabled&&!c.readOnly)})()`;
+    // The Edit button may be pressed before the page's editor is ready: retry.
+    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 10 && !(await browser.evaluate(editable)); attempt++) {
+      await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('[data-editor-toggle]')?.click();true`);
+      await sleep(1000);
+    }
+    await browser.waitFor(editable, 'edit mode');
+    await sleep(1000);

+  // The record page builds the patient summary from these registration fields.

+    'scope:registration:id:web_age': age, 'scope:registration:id:web_date': today,
+  });
+  // Existing required fields of the record (allergy substance, problem list ...)
+  // get synthetic text, so the save is decided only by the medication rules.

+    window.BorkeoRequiredFields?.validate(scope);
+    document.querySelectorAll('.emr-required-missing').forEach(e=>{if('value' in e && !e.value){e.value='Synthetic';e.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));e.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}});
+    return window.BorkeoRequiredFields?.validate(scope)!==false})()`);

+    const sixLines = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => ({ ...oldLine, name: `Old saved line ${i + 1}` }));
+    db.upsertPatient({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), patientKey: ipdKey, userId: admin, savedAt: Date.now(),
+      summary: { caseNumber: 'V423-IPD', healthId: 'H-V423-IPD', firstName: 'Ipd', lastName: 'Synthetic', fullName: 'Ipd Synthetic', sex: 'ប្រុស', age: '45', dateOfBirth: '', phone: '', equityCard: '', paymentType: 'DIRECT', departmentCode: 'medicine', departmentNameKh: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺទូទៅ', departmentNameEn: 'Medicine', serviceMode: 'ipd', entryDate: today, dischargeDate: '' },
+      data: { ...registrationFields('V423-IPD', 'Ipd', '45'), 'system:triageStatus': 'COMPLETED', 'system:admissionStatus': 'admitted', 'system:currentWardCode': 'medicine',
+        'scope:medical:id:ipd_first_exam_date': today, 'system:initialIpdMedications': JSON.stringify(sixLines) } });
+
+    // ---------- IPD admission prescription ----------
+    await useAccount('doctor_medicine');
+    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(ipdKey)}&view=medical`, `document.querySelectorAll('#rx_tbody tr.rx-main-row').length>=6`);
+    let rows = await rowState('#rx_tbody tr.rx-main-row', 'rx');
+    assert.equal(rows.length, 6, 'a saved record with 6 lines shows all 6');
+    assert.deepEqual([rows[0].name, rows[0].unit, rows[0].route, rows[0].dose], ['Old saved line 1', 'mg', 'po', '1'], 'old line: unit shown as mg, route PO, dose 1');
+    await browser.evaluate(`addRxRow(true);true`);
+    rows = await rowState('#rx_tbody tr.rx-main-row', 'rx');
+    assert.deepEqual([rows[6].dose, rows[6].route, rows[6].total], ['', '', '0'], '"+" row: blank number and route, total 0 (no NaN)');
+    const ipdLabel = labelOf(await choosePar('#rx_tbody tr.rx-main-row .rx-name'));
+    rows = await rowState('#rx_tbody tr.rx-main-row', 'rx');
+    let chosen = rows.at(-1);
+    assert.equal(chosen.name, ipdLabel, 'name box gets CMS code-Name-Form-Strength');
+    assert.equal(chosen.unit, expectedUnit(ipdLabel));
+    assert.deepEqual([chosen.dose, chosen.route, chosen.focused], ['', '', true], 'number cleared and focused; route not filled');
+    assert.notEqual(chosen.total, 'NaN');
+    await browser.type('2');
+    // Save without a route: refused with the Khmer message next to the row.
+    assert.equal(await fillRequired(), true, 'required record fields filled');
+    assert.equal(await browser.evaluate(`window.savePatientRecord()`), false);
+    rows = await rowState('#rx_tbody tr.rx-main-row', 'rx');
+    assert.equal(rows.at(-1).routeMessage, 'សូមជ្រើសរើសផ្លូវប្រើថ្នាំ');
+    assert.equal(rows[0].routeMessage, '', 'rows with a route are fine; empty rows are ignored');
+    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const s=[...document.querySelectorAll('#rx_tbody tr.rx-main-row .rx-route')].at(-1);s.value='po';s.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));const t=[...document.querySelectorAll('#rx_tbody tr.rx-main-row .med-schedule-type')].at(-1);t.value='bid';handleMedicationScheduleChange(t);return true})()`);
+    assert.equal(await browser.evaluate(`window.savePatientRecord()`), true, 'saved once a route is chosen');
+    let stored = JSON.parse(JSON.parse(db.findPatient(ipdKey).record_json)['system:initialIpdMedications']);
+    assert.equal(stored.length, 7);
+    // An old line keeps its values; when the user saves the record again, the
+    // unit is written in the new form (mg) and no cmsCode is added.
+    assert.deepEqual([stored[0].name, stored[0].route, stored[0].doseAmount, stored[0].d, stored[0].total, stored[0].unit, 'cmsCode' in stored[0]],
+      ['Old saved line 1', 'po', '1', '3', '6', 'mg', false]);
+    const ipdItem = catalogue.find(entry => entry.label === ipdLabel);
+    assert.equal(stored[6].cmsCode || '', ipdItem.code, 'cmsCode saved with the chosen medicine');
+    assert.equal(stored[6].doseAmount, '2');
+    await browser.evaluate(`window.printPrescription('ipd')`);
+    const ipdPrint = await browser.evaluate(`window.__printed.join('\\n')`);
+    assert.match(ipdPrint, new RegExp(ipdItem.name.replace(/[()]/g, '.')), 'printed prescription has the medicine');
+    assert.match(ipdPrint, /\bmg\b/, 'printed unit is mg, not the old Khmer label');
+    // Reopen.
+    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(ipdKey)}&view=medical`, `document.querySelectorAll('#rx_tbody tr.rx-main-row').length>=7`);
+    rows = await rowState('#rx_tbody tr.rx-main-row', 'rx');
+    assert.deepEqual([rows[6].name, rows[6].cms, rows[6].dose, rows[6].route], [ipdLabel, ipdItem.code, '2', 'po'], 'reopened with name, CMS code, dose and route');
+
+    // ---------- Nursing medication chart (MAR) ----------
+    await useAccount('nurse_medicine');
+    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(ipdKey)}&view=nursing`, `Boolean(document.getElementById('sub_nursing'))`);
+    await browser.waitFor(`document.getElementById('sub_nursing').innerText.includes('Old saved line 1')`, 'nursing chart medicines');
+    const chart = await browser.evaluate(`document.getElementById('sub_nursing').innerText`);
+    assert.match(chart, new RegExp(ipdItem.name.replace(/[()]/g, '.')), 'the chosen medicine is on the nursing chart');
+    assert.equal(chart.includes('ម.ក្រ'), false, 'old unit labels are shown as mg on the chart');
+    await useAccount('doctor_medicine');
+
+    // ---------- Daily note prescription ----------
+    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(ipdKey)}&view=daily`, `document.querySelectorAll('#mon_rx_tbody tr.mrx-main-row').length===3`);
+    rows = await rowState('#mon_rx_tbody tr.mrx-main-row', 'mrx');
+    assert.equal(rows.length, 3, 'the daily note starts with 3 rows');
+    assert.ok(rows.every(row => row.dose === '' && row.route === ''), 'blank number and route');
+    await browser.evaluate(`addMonRxRow(true);true`);
+    assert.equal((await rowState('#mon_rx_tbody tr.mrx-main-row', 'mrx')).length, 4, '"+" adds a row');
+    const dailyLabel = labelOf(await choosePar('#mon_rx_tbody tr.mrx-main-row:first-child .mrx-name'));
+    await browser.type('1');
+    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{document.getElementById('mon_input_date').value=${JSON.stringify(today + 'T09:00')};
+      for (const id of ['mon_input_temp']) document.getElementById(id).value='37';
+      const s=document.querySelector('#mon_rx_tbody tr.mrx-main-row .mrx-route');s.value='po';s.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
+      const t=document.querySelector('#mon_rx_tbody tr.mrx-main-row .med-schedule-type');t.value='bid';handleMedicationScheduleChange(t);
+      if (window.EmrSoapNote) { for (const [id,v] of [['mon_soap_s','Synthetic S'],['mon_soap_a','Synthetic A']]) { const f=document.getElementById(id); if (f) { f.value=v; f.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true})); } } }
+      return true})()`);
+    await fillRequired();
+    await browser.evaluate(`window.saveDailyMonitoring()`);
+    const savedDaily = () => JSON.parse(JSON.parse(db.findPatient(ipdKey).record_json)['system:dailyMonitoringRecords'] || '[]');
+    for (let i = 0; i < 60 && !savedDaily().length; i++) await sleep(250);
+    const daily = savedDaily();
+    const dailyStatus = await browser.evaluate(`[document.getElementById('emr-record-status')?.textContent, document.querySelectorAll('#daily_monitoring_feed .ipd-mon-card').length, [...document.querySelectorAll('.medication-route-message')].length].join(' | ')`);
+    assert.equal(daily.length, 1, `the daily note is saved (${dailyStatus})`);
+    const dailyItem = catalogue.find(entry => entry.label === dailyLabel);
+    assert.equal(daily[0].medications[0].name, dailyLabel);
+    assert.equal(daily[0].medications[0].cmsCode || '', dailyItem.code);
+    assert.equal(daily[0].medications[0].unit, expectedUnit(dailyLabel));
+    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(ipdKey)}&view=daily`, `document.querySelectorAll('#daily_monitoring_feed .ipd-mon-card').length===1`);
+    assert.match(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#daily_monitoring_feed .ipd-mon-card').innerText`), new RegExp(dailyItem.name.replace(/[()]/g, '.')), 'reopened note shows the medicine');
+    await browser.evaluate(`window.__printed=[];printMonitoringRecord(document.querySelector('#daily_monitoring_feed .ipd-mon-card button[onclick*="printMonitoringRecord"]'));true`);
+    const dailyPrint = await browser.evaluate(`document.getElementById('monitoring-print-layout')?.innerText || ''`);
+    assert.match(dailyPrint, new RegExp(dailyItem.name.replace(/[()]/g, '.')), 'printed daily note has the medicine');
+
+    // ---------- Discharge medicines ----------
+    // The discharge list starts as a copy of the latest daily note (existing behaviour).
+    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(ipdKey)}&view=discharge`, `document.querySelectorAll('#emr-discharge-rx-body tr').length===1`);
+    rows = await rowState('#emr-discharge-rx-body tr', '');
+    assert.deepEqual([rows[0].name, rows[0].route], [dailyLabel, 'po'], 'copied from the latest daily note');
+    await sleep(2000);
+    // The discharge form opens in view mode; the doctor presses Edit (v37.8).
+    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{if(document.getElementById('emr-discharge-add-rx').disabled)document.querySelector('[data-editor-toggle]')?.click();return true})()`);
+    await browser.waitFor(`!document.getElementById('emr-discharge-add-rx').disabled`, 'discharge edit mode');
+    await sleep(1000);
+    await browser.evaluate(`document.getElementById('emr-discharge-add-rx').click();true`);
+    await browser.waitFor(`document.querySelectorAll('#emr-discharge-rx-body tr').length===2`, 'discharge "+" row');
+    rows = await rowState('#emr-discharge-rx-body tr', '');
+    assert.deepEqual([rows.length, rows[1].route, rows[1].dose], [2, '', ''], '"+" discharge row: blank route and number');
+    const dischargeLabel = labelOf(await choosePar('#emr-discharge-rx-body tr [data-med="name"]'));
+    rows = await rowState('#emr-discharge-rx-body tr', '');
+    assert.deepEqual([rows[1].name, rows[1].unit, rows[1].dose, rows[1].route, rows[1].focused], [dischargeLabel, expectedUnit(dischargeLabel), '', '', true]);
+    await browser.type('1');
+    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const v=(id,x)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e){e.value=x;e.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));e.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}};
+      v('emr_discharge_datetime',${JSON.stringify(today + 'T10:00')});v('emr_discharge_diagnosis','Synthetic diagnosis');
+      const o=document.getElementById('emr_discharge_outcome');o.value=o.options[1].value;o.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
+      const s=document.getElementById('emr_discharge_status');s.value=s.options[1].value;s.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
+      document.getElementById('emr-save-discharge').click();return true})()`);
+    await sleep(800);
+    rows = await rowState('#emr-discharge-rx-body tr', '');
+    assert.equal(rows[1].routeMessage, 'សូមជ្រើសរើសផ្លូវប្រើថ្នាំ', 'discharge medicine without a route is refused');
+    assert.equal(rows[0].routeMessage, '');
+    assert.equal(JSON.parse(db.findPatient(ipdKey).record_json)['system:discharge'], undefined, 'not saved');
+    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const r=[...document.querySelectorAll('#emr-discharge-rx-body tr [data-med="route"]')].at(-1);r.value='po';r.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));document.getElementById('emr-save-discharge').click();return true})()`);
+    await browser.waitFor(`document.getElementById('emr-discharge-status')?.dataset.state==='saved'`, 'discharge saved');
+    const discharge = JSON.parse(db.findPatient(ipdKey).record_json)['system:discharge'];
+    const dischargeItem = catalogue.find(entry => entry.label === dischargeLabel);
+    assert.equal(discharge.medications.length, 2);
+    assert.equal(discharge.medications[1].name, dischargeLabel);
+    assert.equal(discharge.medications[1].cmsCode || '', dischargeItem.code);
+    assert.equal(discharge.medications[1].doseAmount, '1');
+    assert.equal(discharge.medications[0].cmsCode || '', dailyItem.code, 'the copied daily line keeps its CMS code');
+    await browser.evaluate(`window.__printed=[];window.printDischargeSummary();true`);
+    assert.match(await browser.evaluate(`window.__printed.join('\\n')`), new RegExp(dischargeItem.name.replace(/[()]/g, '.')), 'printed discharge summary has the medicine');
+    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(ipdKey)}&view=discharge`, `document.querySelectorAll('#emr-discharge-rx-body tr').length>0`);
+    rows = await rowState('#emr-discharge-rx-body tr', '');
+    assert.deepEqual([rows[1].name, rows[1].cms, rows[1].route], [dischargeLabel, dischargeItem.code, 'po'], 'reopened discharge medicine');
+
+    // ---------- OPD prescription ----------
+    const opdKey = 'case:V423-OPD';
+    const registration = await api('registration'), triage = await api('triage'), clerk = await api('clerk');
+    const opdReg = { caseNumber: 'V423-OPD', healthId: 'H-V423-OPD', firstName: 'Opd', lastName: 'Synthetic', fullName: 'Opd Synthetic', sex: 'ស្រី', age: '30', dateOfBirth: '', phone: '', equityCard: '', paymentType: 'CASH', departmentCode: '', departmentNameKh: '', departmentNameEn: '', serviceMode: '', entryDate: today, dischargeDate: '' };
+    assert.equal((await fetch(origin + '/api/patient-record', { method: 'PUT', headers: registration, body: JSON.stringify({ patientKey: opdKey, summary: opdReg, data: { ...registrationFields('V423-OPD', 'Opd', '30'), 'scope:registration:id:emr_pmrs_case_new': true } }) })).status, 200);
+    const version = (await (await fetch(origin + `/api/patient-record?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(opdKey)}`, { headers: registration })).json()).patient.version;
+    const opdSummary = { ...opdReg, departmentCode: 'opd', departmentNameKh: 'ពិគ្រោះក្រៅ', departmentNameEn: 'OPD', serviceMode: 'opd' };
+    assert.equal((await fetch(origin + '/api/patient-record', { method: 'PUT', headers: triage, body: JSON.stringify({ patientKey: opdKey, summary: opdSummary, expectedVersion: version, data: { 'scope:triage:id:p2_temp': '37.1' } }) })).status, 200);
+    const bill = (await (await fetch(origin + `/api/finance/patient?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(opdKey)}`, { headers: clerk })).json()).bill;
+    assert.equal((await fetch(origin + '/api/finance/payments', { method: 'POST', headers: clerk, body: JSON.stringify({ patientKey: opdKey, invoiceId: bill.invoice.id, source: 'CASH', amount: bill.summary.KHR.balance, currency: 'KHR' }) })).status, 201);
+    await useAccount('doctor_opd');
+    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(opdKey)}&view=opd`, `document.querySelectorAll('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row').length===3`);
+    rows = await rowState('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row', 'orx');
+    assert.equal(rows.length, 3, 'the OPD prescription starts with 3 rows');
+    assert.ok(rows.every(row => row.dose === '' && row.route === ''), 'blank number and route, no default PO');
+    await ensureEditMode('#opd_rx_tbody .orx-name');
+    await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('[onclick="addOpdRxRow()"]').click();true`);
+    assert.equal((await rowState('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row', 'orx')).length, 4, '"+" adds a row');
+    const opdLabel = labelOf(await choosePar('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row:first-child .orx-name'));
+    await browser.type('1');
+    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const r=document.querySelector('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row .orx-route');r.value='po';r.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
+      const t=document.querySelector('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row .med-schedule-type');t.value='bid';handleMedicationScheduleChange(t);
+      const d=document.querySelector('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row .orx-d');d.value='3';d.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));return true})()`);
+    // Free text is still allowed in another row.
+    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const n=document.querySelectorAll('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row .orx-name')[1];n.focus();return true})()`);
+    await browser.type('Synthetic free text medicine');
+    await browser.key('Escape');
+    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const r=document.querySelectorAll('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row .orx-route')[1];r.value='topical';r.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));return true})()`);
+    await fillRequired();
+    assert.equal(await browser.evaluate(`window.savePatientRecord({submitOpdPrescription:true})`), true, 'OPD prescription saved and sent to Pharmacy');
+    const opdStored = JSON.parse(JSON.parse(db.findPatient(opdKey).record_json)['system:opdMedications']);
+    const opdItem = catalogue.find(entry => entry.label === opdLabel);
+    assert.deepEqual(opdStored.map(med => [med.name, med.cmsCode || '', med.route]), [[opdLabel, opdItem.code, 'po'], ['Synthetic free text medicine', '', 'topical']]);
+    const total = Number(opdStored[0].total);
+    assert.ok(Number.isFinite(total) && total === 6, `total 1 x BID x 3 days = 6 (got ${opdStored[0].total})`);
+    const pharmacy = await api('pharmacy');
+    const list = await (await fetch(origin + '/api/pharmacy/opd-prescriptions', { headers: pharmacy })).json();
+    const rx = list.patients.find(item => item.patient?.healthId === 'H-V423-OPD');
+    assert.ok(rx, 'Pharmacy received the prescription');
+    const detail = await (await fetch(origin + `/api/pharmacy/opd-prescriptions/${rx.id}`, { headers: pharmacy })).json();
+    assert.equal(detail.prescription.medications[0].name, opdLabel, 'Pharmacy sees the full list name');
+    assert.equal((await fetch(origin + `/api/pharmacy/opd-prescriptions/${rx.id}/received`, { method: 'POST', headers: pharmacy })).status, 200, 'dispensed');
+    await browser.evaluate(`window.__printed=[];window.printPrescription('opd');true`);
+    assert.match(await browser.evaluate(`window.__printed.join('\\n')`), new RegExp(opdItem.name.replace(/[()]/g, '.')), 'printed OPD prescription has the medicine');
+    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(opdKey)}&view=opd`, `document.querySelectorAll('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row').length>=3`);
+    rows = await rowState('#opd_rx_tbody tr.orx-main-row', 'orx');
+    assert.deepEqual([rows[0].name, rows[0].cms, rows[0].route, rows[0].dose], [opdLabel, opdItem.code, 'po', '1'], 'reopened OPD prescription');
+    assert.equal(rows.length, 3, '2 saved lines + 1 empty row = 3 rows');
+
+    assert.deepEqual(browser.state.errors, [], 'no JavaScript errors');
+  } finally {
+    browser.close();
+    await new Promise(resolve => app.server.close(resolve));
+    db.close();
+  }
+});
